独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38481213917 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

轻微指引缺失上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
F4 · FilterBuilder 的 string/text 字段能力差异未在指引中说明

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db · @nocobase/repository-input · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    FilterBuilder 类型签名区分 string()/text() 等分组，运行时不匹配会抛 FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED（消息精确指出字段名与类型）。但 nocobase-db 与 database-and-data 指引只演示 collection.string，未说明 text 能力字段必须用 f.text(path)，Agent 需读包源码与类型才能发现。

    实际影响
    搜索类功能容易先写出 f.string 并得到 400，增加一次失败-修复循环。
    建议改法
    在 FilterBuilder/数据库指引中补一句：text 能力字段用 f.text(path)，并给出含 text 字段的搜索示例。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    对 text 字段使用 f.string(path) 构造过滤条件。
    应有能力 / 行为
    指引直接给出按字段类型选择过滤分组的方法。
    实际观察
    指引未提及 FilterBuilder 分组与字段能力的映射，运行时才以 400 暴露。
    应用绕行与代价
    阅读 @nocobase/repository-input 类型签名，改用 f.text(path)。
    改进后的验收标准
    指引包含 string/text 能力对照与可运行示例，静态即可选对方法。
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条

            path: [...path, 'operator'],

    FilterBuilder 类型签名区分 string()/text()/number()/date() 等，string 与 text 是不同能力。

    import type { BooleanFilterOperators, DateFilterOperators, FilterBuilder, FilterConditionNode, FilterFieldGroup, FilterGroupNode, FilterNode, FilterVariable, JsonFilterOperators, NumberFilterOperators, RelationFilterOperators, RepositoryPath, RepositoryRecord, StringFilterOperators, TextFilterOperators, TimeFilterOperators } from './types.js';
export declare class DefaultFilterBuilder<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord> implements FilterBuilder<TRecord> {
    and(items: readonly FilterNode[]): FilterGroupNode;
    or(items: readonly FilterNode[]): FilterGroupNode;
    variable(path: string): FilterVariable;
    string(path: string | RepositoryPath): StringFilterOperators;
    text(path: string | RepositoryPath): TextFilterOperators;
    number(path: string | RepositoryPath): NumberFilterOperators;
    date(path: string | RepositoryPath): DateFilterOperators;
    time(path: string | RepositoryPath): TimeFilterOperators;
    boolean(path: string | RepositoryPath): BooleanFilterOperators;
    json(path: string | RepositoryPath): JsonFilterOperators;
    relation<TTarget extends object = RepositoryRecord>(path: string | RepositoryPath): RelationFilterOperators<TTarget>;
}
export declare function getFilterFieldGroup(node: FilterConditionNode): FilterFieldGroup | undefined;

    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [轻微][指引缺失] FilterBuilder 的 string/text 字段能力差异未在指引中说明

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`@nocobase/repository-input`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.2）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

FilterBuilder 类型签名区分 string()/text() 等分组，运行时不匹配会抛 FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED（消息精确指出字段名与类型）。但 nocobase-db 与 database-and-data 指引只演示 collection.string，未说明 text 能力字段必须用 f.text(path)，Agent 需读包源码与类型才能发现。

**影响**：搜索类功能容易先写出 f.string 并得到 400，增加一次失败-修复循环。

**建议改动**：在 FilterBuilder/数据库指引中补一句：text 能力字段用 f.text(path)，并给出含 text 字段的搜索示例。

**验收标准**：指引包含 string/text 能力对照与可运行示例，静态即可选对方法。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:1055–1075` — 过滤节点校验：当 FilterBuilder 分组与字段类型不匹配时抛 FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED，消息精确指出字段名与类型。
- `packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/filter-builder.d.ts:1–16` — FilterBuilder 类型签名区分 string()/text()/number()/date() 等，string 与 text 是不同能力。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:95–130` — nocobase-db 指引演示 collection.string 等字段声明，但未说明 FilterBuilder 的 string/text 能力差异。
- `artifacts/retro.json:1–45` — 修复轮复盘：实现轮未落地纵切、构建依赖放错、提醒重置超时基准、text 字段过滤、member 角色五处问题与结论。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38481213917 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
    轻微指引缺失上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
    F5 · 默认权限集与可指派应用角色的边界缺少示例

    文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-users · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

    ⌄

    反馈证据核对

    定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

      反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

      应用用户角色经 createMember 的 roleScopes 校验，只接受 app 作用域内可指派的权限集；'member' 是内置默认权限集，传入会得到 INVALID_ROLE_SCOPE_VALUE 'Unknown application role: member'。授权 SKILL 说明了权限集与作用域，但未给出“创建用户并分配角色”的最小示例或可指派角色清单来源。

      实际影响
      团队/用户创建页面容易选到不可指派角色并得到 400。
      建议改法
      在 app-plugin-users 或授权指引中给出最小 Team 页面示例：角色选项读取 app 作用域可指派权限集，并说明 defaultSet 与可指派角色的区别。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      创建用户时传入内置默认权限集（如 member）作为角色。
      应有能力 / 行为
      指引说明哪些 key 可指派、如何枚举可指派权限集。
      实际观察
      仅在运行时以 400 暴露，需自行推断可选项。
      应用绕行与代价
      改为只列 app 作用域可指派的权限集（it-engineer、it-service-desk 等）。
      改进后的验收标准
      指引提供可复制的最小示例与可指派角色枚举方式。
      评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
      packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/services/permission-set-scope.js:105–124E122

      requireDirectRole 对未知应用角色抛出 INVALID_ROLE_SCOPE_VALUE 'Unknown application role: ${role}'。

              .map((assignment) => assignment.permissionSet));
    return {
        permissionSets: sets.filter(({ key }) => !authenticatedDefaults.has(key) &&
            (permissionSets.protection(key)?.unrestricted === true ||
                permissionSets.protection(key) === undefined)),
        assignments,
    };
}
function requireDirectRole(role, available) {
    if (!available.includes(role)) {
        throw new UserManagementError('INVALID_ROLE_SCOPE_VALUE', `Unknown application role: ${role}`);
    }
}
function multipleRoles(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
        throw new UserManagementError('INVALID_ROLE_SCOPE_VALUE', 'The application role scope accepts a role list');
    }
    return [...value];
}

      packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/services/users.js:95–125E123

      createMember 先 validateCreateRoleScopes，再在事务内通过 roleScopes 的 replace 分配角色。

              const changed = this.services.onRoleScopesChanged;
        if (changed)
            connection.afterCommit(() => changed(userId));
    }
    async create(input) {
        const submitted = input.roleScopes ?? {};
        this.validateCreateRoleScopes(submitted);
        const user = await this.services.database.transaction(async (connection) => {
            const users = this.services.users.withConnection(connection);
            const created = await users.create(input);
            for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(submitted)) {
                await this.requireScope(key, 400).replace(created.id, value, connection);
            }
            if (Object.keys(submitted).length > 0) {
                this.afterRoleScopesCommit(connection, created.id);
            }
            return created;
        });
        return this.withRoleScopes(user, this.services.database.connection());
    }
    async update(userId, input) {
        const user = await this.services.database.transaction(async (connection) => {
            await lockUserForAdministration(connection, userId);
            return this.services.users
                .withConnection(connection)
                .update(userId, input);
        });
        return this.withRoleScopes(user, this.services.database.connection());
    }

      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:55–63E124

      授权实现顺序：迁移、defineDatabasePermission/defineCompositeResource、authz.middleware、客户端 authz/useCan、种子配置、允许/拒绝验证。

      ## Implement in this order

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.
7. Verify denied as well as allowed requests through the production route factory and actual database policies. Then verify the UI with ordinary users and inherited memberships.

      ### [轻微][指引缺失] 默认权限集与可指派应用角色的边界缺少示例

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-users`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.2）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

应用用户角色经 createMember 的 roleScopes 校验，只接受 app 作用域内可指派的权限集；'member' 是内置默认权限集，传入会得到 INVALID_ROLE_SCOPE_VALUE 'Unknown application role: member'。授权 SKILL 说明了权限集与作用域，但未给出“创建用户并分配角色”的最小示例或可指派角色清单来源。

**影响**：团队/用户创建页面容易选到不可指派角色并得到 400。

**建议改动**：在 app-plugin-users 或授权指引中给出最小 Team 页面示例：角色选项读取 app 作用域可指派权限集，并说明 defaultSet 与可指派角色的区别。

**验收标准**：指引提供可复制的最小示例与可指派角色枚举方式。

      F3 · 自转换会重置 changedAtField，使“状态时间”与“提醒时间”互相干扰

      NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/lifecycle · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md · 原评审：未解决

      ⌄

      反馈证据核对

      定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

        反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

        plan.js 在每次转移（含 from=to 的自转换）都无条件写 changedAtField=now。应用的 24 小时提醒是一个自转换，会重置 statusChangedAt，导致按状态时间衡量的“超时”工单在提醒后从看板消失；应用改用 createdAt 规避。

        实际影响
        用 changedAtField 表达“进入状态的时间”时，任何自转换/提醒都会破坏该语义。
        建议改法
        允许触发器或转移声明不更新 changedAtField，或提供独立的“最近状态变更时间”与“最近触发时间”字段。
        诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
        触发条件
        对处于某状态的记录执行自转换（如提醒）后，再按 changedAtField 计算“停留时长/超时”。
        应有能力 / 行为
        提醒不应改变“进入状态的时间”，超时判定保持稳定。
        实际观察
        changedAtField 被无条件刷新，超时记录在提醒后不再满足条件。
        应用绕行与代价
        改用 createdAt 或另设字段衡量超时。
        改进后的验收标准
        自转换/触发器可选择保留 changedAtField，并有测试覆盖提醒后超时列表不丢记录。

            throw new LifecycleError('INVALID_SET', `"${transitionName}" may not set "${field}"; the lifecycle owns it.`);
    const version = versionOf(lifecycle, record);
    const nextVersion = (version ?? 0) + 1;
    return {
        transition: transitionName,
        from,
        to,
        input,
        version,
        nextVersion,
        values: {
            ...extra,
            [lifecycle.stateField]: to,
            [lifecycle.changedAtField]: context.now.toISOString(),
            [lifecycle.versionField]: nextVersion,
        },
        effects: [...transition.effects, ...(lifecycle.onEnter.get(to) ?? [])],

        runTriggers 依据 changedAtField 计算 idle 记录并条件更新触发转移，多实例并发安全。

            async runTriggers() {
        let fired = 0;
        const failures = [];
        for (const { lifecycle } of this.lifecycles.values()) {
            const parameters = this.parameters(lifecycle.name);
            for (const trigger of lifecycle.triggers.values()) {
                const changedBefore = new Date(this.clock().getTime() - trigger.definition.after(parameters)).toISOString();
                let after;
                let firedHere = 0;
                while (firedHere < trigger.batchSize) {
                    const records = await this.store.findIdleRecords(lifecycle.collection, {
                        stateField: lifecycle.stateField,
                        states: trigger.when,
                        changedAtField: lifecycle.changedAtField,
                        changedBefore,
                        limit: trigger.batchSize,
                        ...(after ? { after } : {}),
                    });
                    for (const record of records) {
                        if (firedHere >= trigger.batchSize)
                            break;
                        try {
                            // The record must still be idle when the transition is
                            // decided: another sweep may have moved it since this one read it.
                            await this.fire(lifecycle.name, record.id, trigger.transition, {
                                actor: SYSTEM_ACTOR,
                                expect: { changedBefore },
                            });
                            this.triggerRefusals.delete(triggerKey(lifecycle.name, trigger.name, record.id));
                            firedHere += 1;
                        }
                        catch (error) {
                            // Another sweep or a person got there first, or the guard said
                            // no: the record is no longer this trigger's business, and
                            // nothing is logged, since a guard may refuse the same record
                            // on every sweep by design. A refusal from inside the
                            // transition — a lifecycle call its onTransition or a hook made
                            // — is said aloud instead, so it does not fail unseen forever,
                            // on the 1st, 2nd, 4th… sweep it recurs on.
                            if (isRefusal(error, RACED)) {
                                if (raisedInStep.has(error)) {
                                    const sweeps = this.countTriggerRefusal(triggerKey(lifecycle.name, trigger.name, record.id), error.code);
                                    if (isLogworthy(sweeps))
                                        this.logger.warn(`Trigger "${trigger.name}" skipped "${trigger.transition}" on ${lifecycle.name} record "${String(record.id)}": ${error.message}`, {
                                            lifecycle: lifecycle.name,
                                            recordId: String(record.id),
                                            transition: trigger.transition,
        app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:161–183E105

        指引明确：自转换 from=to 会移动 version 并重启触发等待；空闲触发用 when+after；并建议 (status, statusChangedAt) 索引。

        | Record a change without changing the state (reassign, escalate)   | A self-transition, `from` equal to `to`; the version moves and a trigger's wait restarts                                    |
| Available from almost any state                                   | `from: '*'` or `from: { except: [...] }`                                                                                    |
| Something happens after N hours idle                              | A trigger with `when` and `after(parameters)`, firing a transition marked `manual: false`                                   |
| Call an outside system, then move on                              | An effect with `retry`, `timeoutMs`, `onSuccess` / `onFailure`; throw `EffectFailure(code, message, { details })` to branch |
| Always do X when entering a state, whichever transition got there | `onEnter: { state: [effect] }`                                                                                              |
| Write rows of other tables with the change                        | `onTransition(context)`, in the same transaction, writing through `context.transactionHandle`                               |
| Rows that belong to a stay, such as the tasks a stage opens       | `onEnterState` / `onLeaveState` on the lifecycle or on the state itself                                                     |
| Only server code, a webhook or a vote may conclude it             | `manual: false` on the transition                                                                                           |
| Several records move together                                     | `runtime.transaction(async (tx) => { … })`                                                                                  |
| Who may create a record and what it must hold                     | `create: { validate, guard }` on the definition, not in a route, so imports and scripts meet the same rule                  |

`guard`, `route` and `set` must be pure decisions over the record, input, actor, parameters and transaction-bound services. A guard is also asked with `{}` as input by `view()` and `available()`, so it must answer for empty input.

## 2. The migration

The library ships no migration. Write one self-contained migration under `database/main/migrations/` with every field spelled out, as [migrations and seeds](migrations.md) requires: never build it from the lifecycle definition.

The record table needs, besides its business fields:

- the state field, `status` by default, `string` not null;
- `statusChangedAt`, `datetimeTz` not null;
- `lifecycleVersion`, `integer` not null, default `0`;
- an index on `(status, statusChangedAt)`, which the trigger sweep reads.
        app/server/it-service-desk/service.ts:465–495E163

        看板 overdue 改为按 createdAt 计算，并注释说明 24 小时提醒是自转换会重置状态时间戳。

            const now = Date.now();
    const pendingAssignment: TicketRow[] = [];
    const overdue: TicketRow[] = [];
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (row.status in counts)
        counts[row.status as TicketState] =
          counts[row.status as TicketState] + 1;
      if (row.status === 'pending' && !row.assigneeId)
        pendingAssignment.push(row);
      // "Unresolved for over 24 hours" is measured from when the ticket was reported, not from its last status
      // change: the 24-hour reminder is itself a transition and would reset the status timestamp, dropping an
      // overdue ticket off the board exactly when it should stay visible until somebody resolves it.
      if (
        (row.status === 'pending' || row.status === 'processing') &&
        now - new Date(row.createdAt).getTime() > OVERDUE_AFTER_MS
      ) {
        overdue.push(row);
      }
    }

      this.toViews(viewer, pendingAssignment.slice(0, 20)),
      this.toViews(viewer, overdue.slice(0, 20)),
      this.workload(),

      counts,
      pendingAssignment: pendingViews,
      overdue: overdueViews,
      workload,

        上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
        ### [轻微][易用性改进] 自转换会重置 changedAtField，使“状态时间”与“提醒时间”互相干扰

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/lifecycle`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

plan.js 在每次转移（含 from=to 的自转换）都无条件写 changedAtField=now。应用的 24 小时提醒是一个自转换，会重置 statusChangedAt，导致按状态时间衡量的“超时”工单在提醒后从看板消失；应用改用 createdAt 规避。

**影响**：用 changedAtField 表达“进入状态的时间”时，任何自转换/提醒都会破坏该语义。

**建议改动**：允许触发器或转移声明不更新 changedAtField，或提供独立的“最近状态变更时间”与“最近触发时间”字段。

**验收标准**：自转换/触发器可选择保留 changedAtField，并有测试覆盖提醒后超时列表不丢记录。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/plan.js:138–160` — planTransition 拒绝 transition 自行设置生命周期拥有的字段，并在每次转移时无条件写入 changedAtField=now 与递增 version。
- `packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/runtime.js:968–1014` — runTriggers 依据 changedAtField 计算 idle 记录并条件更新触发转移，多实例并发安全。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:161–183` — 指引明确：自转换 from=to 会移动 version 并重启触发等待；空闲触发用 when+after；并建议 (status, statusChangedAt) 索引。
- `app/server/it-service-desk/service.ts:465–495` — 看板 overdue 改为按 createdAt 计算，并注释说明 24 小时提醒是自转换会重置状态时间戳。
- `artifacts/retro.json:1–45` — 修复轮复盘：实现轮未落地纵切、构建依赖放错、提醒重置超时基准、text 字段过滤、member 角色五处问题与结论。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38481213917 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
        业务实现与执行环境观察 · 不计入框架得分（2）
        重要未分类问题候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed原评审：已解决
        F6 · 实现轮产物未真正接入应用，直到浏览器验收才暴露

        工厂流程 · @nocobase/app-cli

        ⌄

        未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

        实现轮只创建了迁移与生命周期定义就报告 completed，服务层、路由、客户端页面/路由/文案、种子、通知与 provider 注册全部缺失；流水线按 exitCode/status 判定成功，verify-1 才发现登录后只有 Home、业务系统不存在。修复轮补齐整条纵切后 verify-2 通过。

        实际影响
        浪费一整轮实现与一次验收，交付时间显著增加。
        建议改法
        在实现轮 required-checks 增加结构性校验：server/routes/index.ts 与 server/providers/index.ts 非空、业务路由可加载、启动后业务 API 至少返回 200，失败即视为未完成。

        本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

        E152 E165 E166 E167 E168
        轻微未分类问题候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
        F9 · 文件插件按声明 MIME/大小存储，不校验文件内容

        Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

        ⌄

        未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

        FileRepository.store 以客户端声明 type 作为 contentType 并校验存储大小与上传大小一致，但不做内容嗅探；verify-2 观察到声明为 PNG 的损坏文件被接受并在浏览器显示为 0x0 破图。

        实际影响
        损坏或伪装内容可进入存储与预览；类型/大小白名单需由上层实现。
        建议改法
        若业务要求，应在应用/客户端增加内容校验（magic bytes）与尺寸校验。

        本条未提供结构化触发条件、绕行与回归标准，请按原文和证据复核，不自动补造。

        E140 E141 E142 E167
        实现者过程与运行背景 · 5 个过程问题 / 3 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

        本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

        本轮过程记录

        修复轮：上一次实现只写了迁移、生命周期和一条迁移测试就报告完成，整条业务纵切（服务、路由、页面、种子、通知）从未落地；本轮补齐整条纵切并逐层验证（真实 sqlite 迁移/种子、开发态端到端 API、生产构建、Playwright 浏览器冒烟），同时修掉构建依赖、超时统计和搜索三处真实缺陷。

        问题 01 · 实现阶段

        实现轮声称完成，但业务系统完全不存在

        本任务已处理

        登录后左侧导航只有 Home，主体是 NocoBase 默认欢迎页；/main/tickets 等全部重定向回 /main；账户菜单无管理入口；无示例工单数据，也没有待分派/超时/工作量看板。git status 只显示三个未跟踪文件：迁移、server/it-service-desk/lifecycle.ts、一条迁移测试。

        原因（实现者自述）
        实现 Agent 只创建了数据库迁移和生命周期定义（外加一条迁移测试）就报告 status: completed / exitCode: 0，没有编写服务层、HTTP 路由、客户端页面/路由/文案、示例种子、通知与文件上传，也没有注册 provider。服务端 server/routes/index.ts 与 server/providers/index.ts 仍是空数组，client/routes.ts 仍只声明模板首页。
        处理方式
        保留既有迁移与生命周期定义，围绕它们补齐整条纵切：server/it-service-desk/{tokens,notifier,service}.ts、server/providers/{it-service-desk,index}.ts、server/routes/{schemas,tickets,files,index}.ts、database/main/seeds/202610140002_it_service_desk_demo_data.ts；客户端 client/pages/tickets/*、client/pages/team/index.tsx、首页改造为看板、client/routes.ts、中英文文案；并注册 inbox 通知渠道、在 provider 内以 60s 间隔驱动生命周期触发器。
        影响 / 代价：整轮修复的主要工作量来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 02 · 自动验证

        生产构建失败：server 代码导入的 @nocobase/logging 在 devDependencies

        本任务已处理

        pnpm build 在 “Verify server dependencies” 阶段 exit 1：@nocobase/logging — imported by server code but not in this application's 'dependencies'。

        原因（实现者自述）
        server/providers/it-service-desk.ts 用 createDiagnosticLogger 适配 LifecycleLogger，但 @nocobase/logging 被放在 devDependencies；dist/package.json 只由 dependencies 生成，部署服务器会缺包。
        处理方式
        把 @nocobase/logging 移到 dependencies 并更新 pnpm-lock.yaml；pnpm build 通过（Verified 25 package(s)）。
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 03 · 自动验证

        24 小时提醒会把工单从“超时”看板上抹掉

        本任务已处理

        服务台看板 overdue 原本按 statusChangedAt 计算；一条超时工单被 remind 自转换提醒一次后，statusChangedAt 被刷新，于是它不再出现在超时列表里，直到解决为止都不再可见。

        原因（实现者自述）
        lifecycle 的 fire 总会写 changedAtField，remind 是 route: record.status 的自转换，因此会重置计时基准；按 statusChangedAt 衡量“未解决超过 24 小时”与提醒机制自相矛盾。
        处理方式
        看板 overdue 改为按 createdAt（报修时间）计算，并用真实数据库验证提醒后的工单仍留在超时列表。
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 04 · 自动验证

        工单搜索 400：对 text 字段用了 f.string

        本任务已处理

        GET /api/tickets?q=printer 返回 400 FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED。

        原因（实现者自述）
        tickets.description 是 text 字段，FilterBuilder 的 string(path) 不支持 text 能力；应使用 text(path)（TextFilterOperators）。
        处理方式
        搜索条件改用 f.text('description')，重新验证 q=printer / q=VPN 大小写不敏感均可命中。
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
        问题 05 · 自动验证

        Team 页面用 member 角色创建用户会 400

        本任务已处理

        POST /api/tickets/team 传 roles: ['member'] 返回 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT “Unknown application role: member”。

        原因（实现者自述）
        createMember 以 roleScopes.app 校验已知应用角色，而 'member' 是默认权限集（authorization.permissionSets.defaultSet）而非可指派的应用角色；Team 页面可选角色来自 app 作用域的可指派权限集（it-engineer、it-service-desk 等）。
        处理方式
        Team 页面的角色复选框只列出 app 作用域的可指派权限集；不选角色的新用户仍可登录并只看自己的工单（默认作用域为 own），已在浏览器中验证。
        影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

        实现者补充建议

        来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

        建议 01 · 验收机制

        实现轮缺少“产物是否真的接上应用”的硬校验

        可自动化

        实现 Agent 只写了迁移和生命周期就报告完成，服务/路由/页面/种子全部缺失，而流水线按 exitCode 与 status 判定为成功，直到浏览器验收才发现。

        建议改动

        在实现轮的 required-checks 中增加一条结构性校验：server/routes/index.ts 与 server/providers/index.ts 非空、client/routes.ts 声明的业务路由可加载、以及启动后业务 API 至少返回 200；失败即视为未完成。

        来源：Agent · retro.json

        建议 02 · Skill 与文档

        应说明 text 字段只能用 FilterBuilder.text(path)

        可自动化

        database-and-data / FilterBuilder 文档没有点明 string(path) 对 text 能力字段会 400，容易在搜索功能上踩坑。

        建议改动

        在 FilterBuilder 参考中补一句：text 能力字段用 f.text(path)，并给出一个包含 text 字段的搜索示例。

        来源：Agent · retro.json

        建议 03 · 脚手架默认值

        团队/用户创建页面的角色来源需要明确

        可自动化

        应用没有 Users 管理页，且 createMember 的 roles 只接受 app 作用域可指派权限集，'member' 会 400；缺少可参考的“创建用户并分配角色”的默认页面或示例。

        建议改动

        在 app-plugin-users 或 authorization 的示例中提供一个最小 Team 页面，角色选项直接读 app 作用域可指派权限集，并说明 defaultSet 与可指派角色的区别。

        来源：Agent · retro.json

        查看原始复盘数据

      "suggestedChange": "在 app-plugin-users 或 authorization 的示例中提供一个最小 Team 页面，角色选项直接读 app 作用域可指派权限集，并说明 defaultSet 与可指派角色的区别。",
      "mechanizable": true
    }
  ]

        对 NocoBase3 基础框架（口径 v2）在 IT 服务台业务上的独立评审：生命周期编排、通知投递、授权/记录范围、数据库过滤、文件附件、应用组合与技能指引六个能力单元。修复轮补齐纵切后，verify-2 以真实浏览器通过全部业务流与角色隔离，框架整体可用。主要框架侧改进点：自转换会重置 changedAtField 导致超时基准漂移；FilterBuilder string/text 字段能力与可指派角色清单缺少指引。实现轮未接入应用属工厂流程问题，已由构建依赖校验与浏览器验收暴露并修复。

        映射验收项：C01

        业务首轮 QA报告未通过业务最终轮 QA报告通过

        这是业务验证背景，不直接换算框架分数；首轮失败不会被最终通过覆盖。

        业务验证背景 · 不计入框架评分
        本 Run 验证轮次2
        本 Run 工厂修复轮次1
        已保留验证轮次2

        仅本 Run 已归档记录；工厂修复轮次不等于 Agent 开发中全部试错次数。缺少 verify-1 时不能声称任务首轮通过。

        首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
        轮次范围原始结论失败项
        验证 1全量浏览器 QA报告未通过C01 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。
        验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

        未提供额外说明。

        业务界面观察 · 非框架评分

        NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 4

        做得好的地方
        F1 · 生命周期定义期校验与事务性转移扎实

        NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/lifecycle

        ⌄

        defineLifecycle 在定义期校验终态 hook、不可达状态、多 to 必须有 route、以及生命周期拥有的字段；planTransition 拒绝 transition 设置拥有字段并在每次转移写入 changedAtField 与递增 version；fire 在单事务内落记录、日志与效果。

        提供的帮助
        Agent 能在运行前发现状态机建模错误，并发转移由条件更新与 version 阻止双提交。
        应保留的能力
        继续把定义期校验扩展到 trigger/effect 命名与参数的一致性。
        E101 E102 E104
        做得好的地方
        F2 · 通知发送具备幂等与逐投递重试

        NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/references/sending-notifications.md

        ⌄

        send() 在持久化前整体校验 idempotencyKey、source、messages 与非法属性，用规范化请求指纹在同键不同内容时抛冲突；投递按 delivery 记录状态并可自动/显式重试；in-app 存储按 deliveryId 去重。

        提供的帮助
        业务事件重复触发不会重复通知，且各渠道失败互不影响。
        应保留的能力
        在指引中补充跨进程/多实例下投递重试的观测示例。
        E110 E111 E112 E113 E114
        做得好的地方
        F7 · 构建期“Verify server dependencies”以可操作信息拦截部署缺包

        NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-cli · app/AGENTS.md

        ⌄

        构建在打包后针对已安装树运行 Verify server dependencies，列出被 server 代码导入却不在 dependencies 的包，并解释 dist/package.json 只由 dependencies 生成、devDependency 在部署时缺失。修复轮据此把 @nocobase/logging 移入 dependencies 并通过构建。

        提供的帮助
        把部署期 Cannot find module 提前到构建期并给出根因，避免线上故障。
        应保留的能力
        可在校验输出中同时给出建议移动到的 dependencies 片段，进一步降低修复成本。
        E152 E153 E154 E168
        做得好的地方
        F8 · 授权快照按 (resource, action) 精确裁剪客户端可见性

        NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md

        ⌄

        Authorization.snapshot 对 unrestricted 身份返回全量标记，否则逐个候选 (resource, action) 判定并分组返回；权限集服务 getEffective 解析身份的有效权限集。应用据此映射 own/assigned/all 记录范围，verify-2 实测三类角色数据隔离正确。

        提供的帮助
        客户端不会显示服务端会拒绝的操作，记录范围决策有明确的服务器侧依据。
        应保留的能力
        补充多来源权限集继承/撤销的端到端示例。

`runtime.create()` does: the\n     * history starts with the creation, and the initial state's `onEnter`\n     * effects run before it returns.\n     */\n    start(values?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>, options?: KitFireOptions & {\n        readonly state?: T['state'];\n    }): Promise<T['record']>;\n    get(record: RecordRef): T['record'];\n    /** Changes fields outside any transition, as an edit form would. */\n    update(record: RecordRef, values: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>): T['record'];\n    /** Fires a transition and returns the record once everything it caused has settled. */\n    fire(record: RecordRef, transition: string, input?: JsonObject, options?: KitFireOptions): Promise<T['record']>;\n    available(record: RecordRef, actor?: string | LifecycleActor): {"type":"tool_execution_update","toolCallId":"call_d59ba13daf4347b38889c254","toolName":"bash","args":{"command":"agent-browser skills get core 2>&1 | head -100","timeout":60},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"---\nname: core\ndescription: Core agent-browser usage guide. Read this before running any agent-browser commands. Covers the snapshot-and-ref workflow, navigating pages, interacting with elements (click, fill, type, select), extracting text and data, taking screenshots, managing tabs, handling forms and auth, waiting for content, running multiple browser sessions in parallel, and troubleshooting common failures. Use when the user asks to interact with a website, fill a form, click something, extract data, take a screenshot, log into a site, test a web app, or automate any browser task.\nallowed-tools: Bash(agent-browser:*), Bash(npx agent-browser:*)\n---\n\n# agent-browser core\n\nFast browser automation CLI for AI agents. Chrome/Chromium via CDP, no Playwright or Puppeteer dependency. Accessibility-tree snapshots with compact `@eN` refs let agents interact with pages in ~200-400 tokens instead of parsing raw HTML.\n\nMost normal web tasks (navigate, read, click, fill, extract, screenshot) are covered here. Load a specialized skill when the task falls outside browser web pages — see [When to load another skill](#when-to-load-another-skill).\n\n## The core loop\n\n```bash\nagent-browser open <url>        # 1. Open a page\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 2. See what's on it (interactive elements only)\nagent-browser click @e3         # 3. Act on refs from the snapshot\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 4. Re-snapshot after any page change\n```\n\nRefs (`@e1`, `@e2`, ...) are assigned fresh on every snapshot. They become **stale the moment the page changes** — after clicks that navigate, form submits, dynamic re-renders, dialog opens. Always re-snapshot before your next ref interaction.\n\n## Always use your own session\n\nBefore your first command, set a named session for the whole task:\n\n```bash\nexport AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION=\"$(agent-browser session id --scope worktree --prefix task)\"\n```\n\nThe default (unnamed) session is a single shared browser: it is shared with every other agent on the machine and it persists across conversations, so working in it can hijack another agent's page mid-task or navigate away from something the human left open. Every example below assumes a named session is active. See [Run multiple browsers in parallel](#run-multiple-browsers-in-parallel) and `references/session-management.md`.\n\n## Quickstart\n\n```bash\n# Install once\nnpm i -g agent-browser && agent-browser install\n\n# Linux hosts can install required browser libraries too\nagent-browser install --with-deps\n\n# Take a screenshot of a page\nagent-browser open https://example.com\nagent-browser screenshot home.png\nagent-browser close\n\n# Search, click a result, and capture it\nagent-browser open https://duckduckgo.com\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # find the search box ref\nagent-browser fill @e1 \"agent-browser cli\"\nagent-browser press Enter\nagent-browser wait --load networkidle\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # refs now reflect results\nagent-browser click @e5                        # click a result\nagent-browser screenshot result.png\n```\n\nThe browser stays running across commands so these feel like a single session. By default, an inactive daemon saves configured restore state, closes its headless browser, and exits after one hour; the next command starts it again. Without `--restore` or another restore key, shutdown discards transient browser state and open tabs. Dashboard mouse, keyboard, and touch input count as activity. Headed browsers, Safari and iOS WebDriver sessions, and user-attached browsers are exempt from the default; provider-owned cloud browsers are not. Use `--idle-timeout <time>` or `AGENT_BROWSER_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` to tune the timeout, and use `0` to disable it. Still run `agent-browser close` (or `close --all`) when you're done.\n\n## MCP integration\n\nFor tools that support Model Context Protocol servers, start the stdio server:\n\n```bash\nagent-browser mcp\nagent-browser mcp --tools all\nagent-browser mcp --tools core,network,react\n```\n\nConfigure the MCP client to launch `agent-browser` with `[\"mcp\"]`. The server defaults to MCP protocol 2025-11-25 and accepts older supported client protocol versions during initialization. The default tools profile is `core`, which keeps MCP context small for everyday browser automation. Use `--tools all` for the full typed CLI parity surface, or combine profiles with commas, such as `--tools core,network,react`. Profiles are `core`, `network`, `state`, `debug`, `tabs`, `react`, `mobile`, and `all`; the `debug` profile includes accessibility audits, plugin registry, and command.run tools. Each tool accepts typed arguments plus `extraArgs` for advanced CLI flags and exact CLI parity. The common `allowedDomains` array maps to `--allowed-domains` and activates the same WebRTC containment and launch-mode restrictions, while `idleTimeout` maps to `--idle-timeout`. Tool discovery is paginated and includes read-only/open-world annotations so modern MCP clients can load the large typed surface incrementally. Use the tool `session` argument or `AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION` to isolate browser sessions.\n\n## eve agent integration\n\nFor eve agents, mount the `@agent-browser/eve` extension instead of hand-writing browser tools. It adds namespaced tools such as `browser__navigate`, `browser__snapshot`, `browser__click`, `browser__fill`, `browser__find`, and `browser__screenshot`, all backed by agent-browser running inside the eve sandbox. The sandbox bootstrap helpers (`installAgentBrowser`, `agentBrowserRevalidationKey`) ship with the same package under `@agent-browser/eve/sandbox`, so `agent/sandbox.ts` needs no extra dependency.\n\n## Reading a page\n\n```bash\nagent-browser snapshot                    # full tree (verbose)\nagent-browser snapshot -i                 # interactive elements only (preferred)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -u              # include href urls on links\nagent-browser snapshot -i -c              # compact (no empty structural nodes)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -d 3            # cap depth at 3 levels\nagent-browser snapshot -s \"#main\"         # scope to a CSS selector\nagent-browser snapshot -i --json          # machine-readable output\n```\n\nSnapshot output looks like:\n\n```\nPage: Example - Log in\nURL: https://example.com/login\n\n@e1 [heading] \"Log in\"\n@e2 [form]\n  @e3 [input type=\"email\"] placeholder=\"Email\"\n  @e4 [input type=\"password\"] placeholder=\"Password\"\n  @e5 [button type=\"submit\"] \"Continue\"\n  @e6 [link] \"Forgot password?\"\n"}],"details":{}}}

        E101 · packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/README.md:37–54

        生命周期概念表（transition/effect/trigger）、事务性 fire、dispatcher、runTriggers/reclaim/recover，以及各入口包说明。

        | Concept    | What it is                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Transition | `from`, `to`, and optionally `guard` (may this actor fire it, and if not, why), `validate` (input problems), `accept` (input fields written onto the record), `route` (pick one of several `to`) and `set` (other fields)                                      |
| Effect     | A function run after commit, with `retry`, a stable `idempotencyKey`, and `onSuccess`/`onFailure` transitions, fired with the effect's result or its error — a code and details when it throws an `EffectFailure` — as input; `onEnter` runs effects per state |
| Trigger    | Fires a transition on records idle in a state for longer than `after`, found by a query rather than a timer per record                                                                                                                                         |

`LifecycleRuntime.fire()` checks the state, writes the record, a transition log entry and the effect runs in one transaction, guarded by a conditional update on the state and on a version every transition increments, so two concurrent transitions of one record cannot both commit. Effects are handed to a dispatcher after commit, retried by policy, and fenced so a reclaimed attempt cannot record a result over its replacement. `runTriggers()` sweeps the triggers, `reclaim()` hands over again what no process is working on — attempts whose lease expired and queued runs whose dispatch was lost — and tries again a waiting continuation once it is due, and `recover()` picks up what an earlier process left queued.

## Entries

| Entry                         | Provides                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `@nocobase/lifecycle`         | `defineLifecycle`, `defineEffect`, `LifecycleRuntime`, `createRepositoryLifecycleStore`, `MemoryLifecycleStore`, `toMermaid`, and `lifecycleErrorFields()` and `lifecycleDescriptionView()` for a plugin's own routes |
| `@nocobase/lifecycle/jobs`    | `createLifecycleJobs()`, the dispatcher that runs effects on `@nocobase/jobs` and sweeps triggers on a schedule                                                                                                       |
| `@nocobase/lifecycle/react`   | `createLifecycleHook()`, configured once per plugin so a page calls one hook; `createLifecycleClient()` and `useLifecycle()` beneath it                                                                               |
| `@nocobase/lifecycle/testing` | `createLifecycleTestKit()`: a memory store, a fake clock and in-process effects whose retries wait for that clock, so waits and retries are unit tests                                                                |

`@nocobase/db` is a peer; `@nocobase/jobs` and `react` are optional peers, each needed only by the entry that imports it. A plugin declares `@nocobase/lifecycle` itself in `peerDependencies`, never in `dependencies`, and the application provides it in its own `dependencies`: the plugins of an application share one runtime, and code recognises `LifecycleError` and `EffectFailure` with `instanceof`, which a second copy of the module fails. The library ships no HTTP routes: they belong to the plugin, under its namespace and declared for the application's API document, and the guide shows how to write the ones the React hook calls.

        E103 · packages/@nocobase/lifecycle/dist/runtime.js:968–1014

        runTriggers 依据 changedAtField 计算 idle 记录并条件更新触发转移，多实例并发安全。

            async runTriggers() {
        let fired = 0;
        const failures = [];
        for (const { lifecycle } of this.lifecycles.values()) {
            const parameters = this.parameters(lifecycle.name);
            for (const trigger of lifecycle.triggers.values()) {
                const changedBefore = new Date(this.clock().getTime() - trigger.definition.after(parameters)).toISOString();
                let after;
                let firedHere = 0;
                while (firedHere < trigger.batchSize) {
                    const records = await this.store.findIdleRecords(lifecycle.collection, {
                        stateField: lifecycle.stateField,
                        states: trigger.when,
                        changedAtField: lifecycle.changedAtField,
                        changedBefore,
                        limit: trigger.batchSize,
                        ...(after ? { after } : {}),
                    });
                    for (const record of records) {
                        if (firedHere >= trigger.batchSize)
                            break;
                        try {
                            // The record must still be idle when the transition is
                            // decided: another sweep may have moved it since this one read it.
                            await this.fire(lifecycle.name, record.id, trigger.transition, {
                                actor: SYSTEM_ACTOR,
                                expect: { changedBefore },
                            });
                            this.triggerRefusals.delete(triggerKey(lifecycle.name, trigger.name, record.id));
                            firedHere += 1;
                        }
                        catch (error) {
                            // Another sweep or a person got there first, or the guard said
                            // no: the record is no longer this trigger's business, and
                            // nothing is logged, since a guard may refuse the same record
                            // on every sweep by design. A refusal from inside the
                            // transition — a lifecycle call its onTransition or a hook made
                            // — is said aloud instead, so it does not fail unseen forever,
                            // on the 1st, 2nd, 4th… sweep it recurs on.
                            if (isRefusal(error, RACED)) {
                                if (raisedInStep.has(error)) {
                                    const sweeps = this.countTriggerRefusal(triggerKey(lifecycle.name, trigger.name, record.id), error.code);
                                    if (isLogworthy(sweeps))
                                        this.logger.warn(`Trigger "${trigger.name}" skipped "${trigger.transition}" on ${lifecycle.name} record "${String(record.id)}": ${error.message}`, {

                                            recordId: String(record.id),

        E105 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:161–183

        指引明确：自转换 from=to 会移动 version 并重启触发等待；空闲触发用 when+after；并建议 (status, statusChangedAt) 索引。

        | Record a change without changing the state (reassign, escalate)   | A self-transition, `from` equal to `to`; the version moves and a trigger's wait restarts                                    |
| Available from almost any state                                   | `from: '*'` or `from: { except: [...] }`                                                                                    |
| Something happens after N hours idle                              | A trigger with `when` and `after(parameters)`, firing a transition marked `manual: false`                                   |
| Call an outside system, then move on                              | An effect with `retry`, `timeoutMs`, `onSuccess` / `onFailure`; throw `EffectFailure(code, message, { details })` to branch |
| Always do X when entering a state, whichever transition got there | `onEnter: { state: [effect] }`                                                                                              |
| Write rows of other tables with the change                        | `onTransition(context)`, in the same transaction, writing through `context.transactionHandle`                               |
| Rows that belong to a stay, such as the tasks a stage opens       | `onEnterState` / `onLeaveState` on the lifecycle or on the state itself                                                     |
| Only server code, a webhook or a vote may conclude it             | `manual: false` on the transition                                                                                           |
| Several records move together                                     | `runtime.transaction(async (tx) => { … })`                                                                                  |
| Who may create a record and what it must hold                     | `create: { validate, guard }` on the definition, not in a route, so imports and scripts meet the same rule                  |

`guard`, `route` and `set` must be pure decisions over the record, input, actor, parameters and transaction-bound services. A guard is also asked with `{}` as input by `view()` and `available()`, so it must answer for empty input.

## 2. The migration

The library ships no migration. Write one self-contained migration under `database/main/migrations/` with every field spelled out, as [migrations and seeds](migrations.md) requires: never build it from the lifecycle definition.

The record table needs, besides its business fields:

- the state field, `status` by default, `string` not null;
- `statusChangedAt`, `datetimeTz` not null;
- `lifecycleVersion`, `integer` not null, default `0`;
- an index on `(status, statusChangedAt)`, which the trigger sweep reads.

        E106 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/lifecycle.md:228–256

        指引给出 createLifecycleJobs 的 dispatcher 配置、sweepEveryMs 与多实例 jobs.default 说明。

              this.lifecycleRuntime(),
    );

  public override async start(): Promise<void> {
    // Registers the effect job, opens the executors, schedules the sweep
    // (reclaim, triggers, onSweep) and recovers runs an earlier process left.
    await this.effectJobs().start(this.lifecycleRuntime());
  }

  public override async shutdown(): Promise<void> {
    await this.jobs?.shutdown();
  }

  private effectJobs(): LifecycleJobs {
    if (this.jobs) return this.jobs;
    const executors = this.app.container.resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken);
    this.jobs = createLifecycleJobs({
      jobs: executors.getJobExecutor(SCOPE),
      schedule: executors.getScheduleExecutor(SCOPE),
      jobName: `${SCOPE}/lifecycle-effect`, // stored with every queued task: keep it stable
      sweepEveryMs: 60_000, // well below the shortest trigger `after`
      onSweep: async () => {
        await this.lifecycleRuntime().prune({
          olderThan: new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86_400_000),
        });
      },
    });
    return this.jobs;

            (!input.source ||

            'content',
            'routing',

                expandedRecipients.push({
                    channels: [{ channel, provider, recipient }],
                });

            ...input,
            messages: message,
        });

                throw new NotificationIdempotencyConflictError(input.idempotencyKey);
            }
            const existingDeliveries = await this.store.listDeliveries(existing.id);

        E111 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/shared/target.js:1–38

        validateNotificationTarget 对 route/url 目标做白名单校验，拒绝不安全字符与非法 URL。

        export function isNotificationTarget(value) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object')
        return false;
    const target = value;
    if (target.type === 'route') {
        return (typeof target.path === 'string' &&
            target.path.startsWith('/') &&
            !target.path.startsWith('//') &&
            !hasUnsafeCharacters(target.path));
    }
    if (target.type !== 'url' ||
        typeof target.url !== 'string' ||
        !/^https?:\/\//i.test(target.url) ||
        hasUnsafeCharacters(target.url))
        return false;
    try {
        const url = new URL(target.url);
        return Boolean(url.hostname) && !url.username && !url.password;
    }
    catch {
        return false;
    }
}
export function validateNotificationTarget(value) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null)
        return undefined;
    if (!isNotificationTarget(value))
        throw new Error('Invalid notification target: use an internal route path or a complete HTTP(S) URL.');
    return value.type === 'route'
        ? { type: 'route', path: value.path }
        : { type: 'url', url: value.url };
}
function hasUnsafeCharacters(value) {
    return [...value].some((character) => character === '\\' ||
        character.charCodeAt(0) <= 32 ||
        character.charCodeAt(0) === 127);
}

        MemoryInAppStore.deliver 按 deliveryId 去重，并对每条 in-app 消息再次校验 target。

        import { validateNotificationTarget, } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-notification';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
export class MemoryInAppStore {
    items = new Map();
    async deliver(input) {
        const existing = [...this.items.values()].find((item) => item.deliveryId === input.deliveryId);
        if (existing)
            return existing;
        const item = {
            id: randomUUID(),
            deliveryId: input.deliveryId,
            notificationId: input.notificationId,
            userId: input.userId,
            title: input.message.title,
            body: input.message.body,
            target: validateNotificationTarget(input.message.target),
            createdAt: input.createdAt,
            updatedAt: input.createdAt,
        };
        this.items.set(item.id, item);
        return item;
    }
    async list(input) {
        return [...this.items.values()]
            .filter((item) => item.userId === input.userId &&
            (!input.unreadOnly || !item.readAt) &&
            (!input.before || isBefore(item, input.before)))
            .sort(compareItemsDescending)
            .slice(0, input.limit ?? 25);
    }
    async countUnread(userId) {
        return (await this.list({
            userId,
            unreadOnly: true,
            limit: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
        })).length;
    }
    async update(input) {
        const item = this.items.get(input.id);
        if (!item || item.userId !== input.userId)
            return undefined;
        const next = {
            ...item,
            readAt: input.action === 'read'
                ? new Date().toISOString()
                : input.action === 'unread'
                    ? undefined
                    : item.readAt,
            updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };

Email requires a native email address or a non-empty readonly array of addresses, a subject, and text or HTML. Each address receives a separate email and retries independently. In-app messages require application user IDs in the same string/array shape, title, and body; final delivery checks that each user still exists. Webhook messages forbid `to`. The service does not resolve application users into email addresses or phone numbers and never defaults to the current user.

IM targets accept complete HTTP(S) URLs only. In-app targets accept a route without a deployment prefix or a complete HTTP(S) URL. React Router supplies the basename once for routes; URL targets use native links. Email links belong in text/HTML. Legacy `actionUrl` is ignored.

Unknown or disabled Channels and invalid messages or recipient formats reject the entire request before persistence or queue dispatch. Delivery-time failures are recorded independently. Reuse one stable `idempotencyKey` only for the same logical request; changing messages with that key is a conflict. Optional `source` adds business correlation. Use `getByIdempotencyKey`, `getNotification`, and `onStatusChanged` to observe results. A `retrying` Delivery is waiting for automatic execution; a terminal `failed` Delivery may be passed to `retryDelivery({ deliveryId, reason })`. An `unknown` Delivery must be checked externally before creating a new logical send.

        E114 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/server/idempotency.js:1–39

        validateNotificationIdempotencyKey 与 notificationRequestFingerprint（sha256 规范化指纹，前缀 v2:）。

        import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
const IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MAX_LENGTH = 191;
export function validateNotificationIdempotencyKey(value) {
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value || value !== value.trim()) {
        throw new Error('Notification idempotencyKey must be a non-empty trimmed string.');
    }
    if (value.length > IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MAX_LENGTH) {
        throw new Error(`Notification idempotencyKey must not exceed ${IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MAX_LENGTH} characters.`);
    }
}
export function notificationRequestFingerprint(input) {
    const canonical = normalizeValue({
        source: {
            type: input.source?.type ?? 'application',
            referenceId: input.source?.referenceId,
        },
        messages: input.messages,
    });
    return `v2:${createHash('sha256')
        .update(JSON.stringify(canonical))
        .digest('hex')}`;
}
function normalizeValue(value) {
    if (Array.isArray(value))
        return value.map(normalizeValue);
    if (!isRecord(value))
        return value;
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value)
        .filter(([, item]) => item !== undefined)
        .sort(([left], [right]) => compareCanonicalStrings(left, right))
        .map(([key, item]) => [key, normalizeValue(item)]));
}
function compareCanonicalStrings(left, right) {
    return left < right ? -1 : left > right ? 1 : 0;
}
function isRecord(value) {
    return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

            /**
     * Lists exactly the (type, id, action) triples `can()` permits among the
     * identity's expanded grants, so the client never shows what the server
     * refuses.
     */
    async snapshot(identity, grants, constraints) {
        if ((await grants.unrestricted?.(identity)) === true)
            return { unrestricted: true, permissions: [] };
        const candidates = new Map();
        for (const grant of await grants.resolveAll(identity))
            candidates.set(JSON.stringify([grant.resource.type, grant.resource.id, grant.action]), {
                resource: { type: grant.resource.type, id: grant.resource.id },
                action: grant.action,
            });
        const permitted = await Promise.all([...candidates.values()].map(async (candidate) => ({
            candidate,
            permitted: (await this.authorizeWithGrants({ ...identity, ...candidate }, grants, constraints, false)).effect === 'permit',
        })));
        const grouped = new Map();
        for (const { candidate, permitted: allowed } of permitted) {
            if (!allowed)
                continue;
            const key = JSON.stringify([
                candidate.resource.type,
                candidate.resource.id,
            ]);
            const permission = grouped.get(key) ?? {
                resource: candidate.resource,
                actions: new Set(),
            };
            permission.actions.add(candidate.action);
            grouped.set(key, permission);
        }
        return {
            unrestricted: false,
            permissions: [...grouped.entries()]
                .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
                .map(([, permission]) => ({
                resource: permission.resource,
                actions: [...permission.actions].sort(),

        E124 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:55–63

        授权实现顺序：迁移、defineDatabasePermission/defineCompositeResource、authz.middleware、客户端 authz/useCan、种子配置、允许/拒绝验证。

        ## Implement in this order

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.
7. Verify denied as well as allowed requests through the production route factory and actual database policies. Then verify the UI with ordinary users and inherited memberships.

        E133 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-and-data.md:1–20

        数据访问指引：解析 databaseManagerToken，repository/query/transaction 的选择与归属。

        # Database and data access

This page is how an application reaches the database and where that code belongs. The API itself — Repository, QueryAdapter, transactions, naming, and the criterion for choosing between the two layers — belongs to the package that owns it: read `.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md` sections 4 and 5, and run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` if that file is missing. Schema changes are migrations; see [migrations and seeds](migrations.md).

## Resolving the database

Resolve `databaseManagerToken` from the container:

```ts
import { databaseManagerToken } from '@nocobase/db';

const database = app.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
```

In a route factory, resolve it once in the factory rather than inside each handler. In a service, take it as a constructor dependency — see [services and jobs](services-and-jobs.md).

From there, `database.repository(collection)` is the default for a collection's records and their relations, `database.query()` is for a result set that is not any collection's records, and `database.transaction(fn)` groups writes that must succeed or fail together. Each takes an optional connection name and uses the default connection when omitted.

Keep `@nocobase/db` in `dependencies`, not `devDependencies`. The deployed server resolves it at runtime, and TypeScript also reads that declaration to infer the database configuration through the public package name; declaring it only as a development dependency can cause TS2883 in an installed application even while a source workspace builds successfully.

        ```ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609080001_create_invoice_files',
  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('invoice_files', (collection) => {
      collection.uuid('id').primary().notNull();
      collection.string('disk', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.text('key').notNull();
      collection.text('filename').notNull();
      collection.string('ext', { length: 32 }).notNull();
      collection.string('mimeType', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.bigInt('size').notNull();
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();
    });
  },
  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('invoice_files');
  },
});
export default migration;
```

The fields are fixed; mapping is unsupported. The primary key must accept a 36-character UUID, string fields accept string/char/text, size accepts integer/bigInt, and timestamps accept datetime/datetimeTz. Apply with the App's migration command. Never import a live collection schema from a migration.

Runtime code that selects or checks the full column set imports `FILE_COLUMNS` (and its element type `FileColumn`) from `@nocobase/app-plugin-file/server` instead of copying the list, for example `select: (s) => s.fields(...FILE_COLUMNS)`. A migration still spells its columns out as above, because importing the list would let a later release change an already applied migration.

Upload generates the ID and storage key, normalizes the filename/extension, validates stored size, and supplies timestamps. Extra required columns need defaults because upload accepts no business values. `contentUrl` is derived, never persisted. Save returned file IDs through an App-owned relation or link table when submitting the business form; upload and form submission are separate commits.

        E150 · packages/@nocobase/service-provider/dist/registry.js:1–48

        ServiceProviderRegistry 的注册/启动阶段机与同名 provider 拒绝，保证 provider 只在注册前加入。

        export class ServiceProviderRegistry {
    providers = [];
    phase = 'new';
    shutdownPromise;
    add(provider) {
        if (this.phase !== 'new') {
            throw new Error('Providers can only be added before registration.');
        }
        if (this.providers.some(({ provider: item }) => item.name === provider.name)) {
            throw new Error(`Service provider "${provider.name}" is already registered.`);
        }
        this.providers.push({ provider, state: 'new' });
    }
    registerAll() {
        this.assertPhase('new');
        for (const entry of this.providers) {
            entry.state = 'registering';
            try {
                entry.provider.register();
                entry.state = 'registered';
            }
            catch (error) {
                entry.state = 'failed';
                this.phase = 'failed';
                throw error;
            }
        }
        this.phase = 'registered';
    }
    async bootAll() {
        this.assertPhase('registered');
        await this.runPhase('booting', 'booted', (provider) => provider.boot());
        this.phase = 'booted';
    }
    async startAll() {
        this.assertPhase('booted');
        await this.runPhase('starting', 'started', (provider) => provider.start());
        this.phase = 'started';
    }
    async readyAll() {
        this.assertPhase('started');
        await this.runPhase('readying', 'ready', (provider) => provider.ready());
        this.phase = 'ready';
    }
    shutdown() {
        this.shutdownPromise ??= this.shutdownAll();
        return this.shutdownPromise;
    }

        const problems = [];

  //
  // Inside this repository a package can reach `dist/package.json` without being declared here at all: workspace
  // packages are vendored transitively, so `@nocobase/db` arrives as a dependency of `@nocobase/app-server` and the

  // templates, verified clean on every build here, and failed the first time an application was generated from them.

    ]);

  );

}

  'Packages your server code imports will not be usable in a deployment:\n',
);

        E154 · app/AGENTS.md:322–354

        AGENTS.md 明确 server/database/cli 导入放 dependencies，client/构建/测试放 devDependencies，并解释部署期失败方式。

        ## Adding a dependency

Keep `@nocobase/db` in `dependencies` alongside the database driver. It supplies the driver's runtime peer and lets TypeScript resolve the inferred database configuration declaration through the public package name. Moving it to `devDependencies` can cause TS2883 in an installed application even while the source workspace builds successfully.

Put a package your **server** code imports in `dependencies`. Put everything your **client** code imports — along with build tooling, tests, and type-only imports — in `devDependencies`.

That split looks backwards until you see how the two halves are deployed. `pnpm build` bundles the client: Vite resolves every client import and inlines it into `dist/client`, so nothing has to resolve it again later. The server is not bundled. `dist/server` keeps its bare imports, and `pnpm build` generates `dist/package.json` from your `dependencies` and installs a `node_modules` next to it — that tree is what the deployed server resolves against, and `devDependencies` are not in it.

So the two mistakes fail in opposite ways. A server import left in `devDependencies` works all through development and fails only on the server, with a bare `Cannot find package` naming nothing that points back here. A client package put in `dependencies` never breaks anything — it is just installed into every deployment, where the server never requires it. That one is invisible, so it accumulates: `lucide-react` and `@xyflow/react` were 44 MB of it before this rule was written down.

What decides it is where the importing file lives and what the import is, not what the package is for. `import ts from 'typescript'` in `server/` is a runtime dependency even though TypeScript sounds like tooling. `import type { Config } from 'x'` is erased before anything runs, so it stays a devDependency wherever it appears. A dynamic `import()` counts — deferring the load changes when a package is needed, not whether.

### Adding a dependency

Which half of the application imports it decides where it goes.

| The import is reached from              | Declare it in     |
| --------------------------------------- | ----------------- |
| `server/`, `database/`, or `cli/`       | `dependencies`    |
| `client/`, build tooling, tests         | `devDependencies` |
| `import type` only, wherever it appears | `devDependencies` |

`dist/package.json` is generated from `dependencies` and is what a deployment installs from, so a server import declared as a devDependency resolves in every development checkout and is absent exactly once — on the deployed server. A client import needs nothing at runtime: Vite resolves and inlines it into `dist/client` at build time.

A plugin's browser packages arrive by a third route and need nothing from you. Plugins declare those as peer dependencies, so installing a plugin brings one shared copy into this application, while `dist/` sets `autoInstallPeers: false` and installs none of them.

`pnpm build` checks the server half: it reads every value import in `server/`, `database/`, and `cli/` and fails the build if any of those packages is missing from `dist/package.json`. It cannot see an import whose specifier is built at run time:

```ts
await import(`${name}/index.js`); // invisible to the check
```

Declare such a package in `dependencies` when you write the code; nothing will remind you later.

        原文件 SHA-256：03b54a8d615f57144db20921ce6657030440a6577f498fc1c4a21b170d266774

        E155 · app/AGENTS.md:62–70

        AGENTS.md 指出一个带页面与 API 的功能会触及迁移、路由、页面、导航、文案五处，并说明 runtime 组合方式。

        A feature with a page and an API touches five places: a migration for the table, a route in `server/routes/`, a page in `client/pages/` declared in `client/routes.ts`, navigation on the page route, and strings in `client/locales/`.

`client/runtime.ts` composes the browser application; `client/react-providers.ts` declares React providers in outer-to-inner layers `root`, `application`, and `extension`. Applications use the first two and plugins own the last; `before` and `after` order providers only within their layer.

`server/runtime.ts` composes configuration, plugins, providers and routes; `server/app.ts` assembles the application. `server/standalone.ts` starts the Node listener and `server/embedded.ts` lets a host mount the same runtime. Register endpoints through `server/routes/index.ts`; queue consumers and background jobs are registered from a service provider, not discovered from a directory. Editable module defaults live in `server/config/` and are collected by `defaultAppConfigs` in its `index.ts`; `server/config.ts` loads the configuration file. Each section declares the environment variables that set it in `env` of its `defineAppConfig`, with a `description` and, where it applies, `secret`, `required`, `generate` or `firstStartOnly` in the helper's second argument; `pnpm nocobase config env` lists them all, `pnpm nocobase config variables` describes each, and `pnpm build` writes that description to `dist/variables.json` for whoever deploys the build. `server/config/database.ts` maps `DB_*` onto the main connection and `server/config/users.ts` maps `INITIAL_ADMIN_*` onto the initial administrator. `config.yml` is read as written: a `${NAME}` in it is not expanded.

### The rest is framework structure

Layouts own breadcrumb route context and render the current page's trail in the header after the sidebar toggle (`client/components/breadcrumbs.tsx`): the route trail by default, or the trail a page declares with `usePageBreadcrumb` from `@nocobase/app-client` when it names records. Pages do not render breadcrumbs themselves. `AppRouter` selects routes and layouts. See [pages and routes](.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/page.md) for each layout's scope.

        应用 viewer() 读取 authz.snapshot() 与 permissionSets.getEffective()，据此映射 own/assigned/all 记录范围。

          async viewer(authz: AuthorizationContext): Promise<TicketViewer> {
    const identity = authz.identity;
    const actor: LifecycleActor = {
      id: String(identity.principal.id),
      system: false,
    };
    const snapshot = await authz.snapshot();
    let scope: TicketScope = 'own';
    if (snapshot.unrestricted) {
      scope = 'all';
    } else {
      const authorization = this.app.container.resolve(authorizationToken);
      const sets = await authorization.permissionSets.getEffective({
        principal: identity.principal,
        subjects: identity.subjects,
      });
      const keys = new Set(sets.map((set) => set.key));
      if (keys.has(SERVICE_DESK_PERMISSION_SET)) scope = 'all';
      else if (keys.has(ENGINEER_PERMISSION_SET)) scope = 'assigned';
    }
    return { userId: actor.id, scope, actor };
  }

        import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

/**
 * The IT service desk: repair tickets, their attachments, and the two log tables `@nocobase/lifecycle` reads and
 * writes. Every field is spelled out here rather than built from the lifecycle definition or the file plugin's
 * `FILE_COLUMNS`, because a migration is fixed history: a later release changing either must not change what this
 * migration already did.
 *
 * `tickets.status`, `tickets.statusChangedAt` and `tickets.lifecycleVersion` are the lifecycle-owned fields — only
 * `LifecycleRuntime` may write them. `it_ticket_files` carries the file plugin's required columns plus the uploader,
 * which the exposure's record access scopes to the caller.
 */

        E156 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:1–40

        app-server/router 定义 AIP-193 规范化错误码到 HTTP 状态的映射与 ApiError，路由以统一错误体回答。

        import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { RepositoryError } from '@nocobase/db';

import { HTTPException } from 'hono/http-exception';
/**
 * The error categories an `/api` response may report, with the HTTP status each one maps to. The names and mapping are
 * Google's canonical error codes (AIP-193), so a client branches on a fixed, documented set rather than on whatever
 * each plugin invents. `reason` carries the plugin-specific detail.
 */
export const apiErrorStatusCodes = Object.freeze({
    INVALID_ARGUMENT: 400,
    FAILED_PRECONDITION: 400,
    OUT_OF_RANGE: 400,
    UNAUTHENTICATED: 401,
    PERMISSION_DENIED: 403,
    NOT_FOUND: 404,
    ALREADY_EXISTS: 409,
    ABORTED: 409,
    RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED: 429,
    INTERNAL: 500,
    UNIMPLEMENTED: 501,
    UNAVAILABLE: 503,
    DEADLINE_EXCEEDED: 504,
});
/** The error reasons and domain the framework itself reports. Plugins use their own domain and reasons. */
export const appErrorDomain = 'app';
/**
 * An error a route throws to answer with the standard `/api` error body.
 *
 * ```ts
 * throw new ApiError({ status: 'NOT_FOUND', reason: 'WORKFLOW_NOT_FOUND', domain: 'workflows', message: `Workflow ${id} was not found.` });
 * ```
 */
export class ApiError extends Error {
    /** The HTTP status of the response. */
    code;
    status;
    reason;
    domain;
    localizedMessage;

        业务场景与交付说明

        报修到关闭全流程

        员工提交标题、描述、紧急程度和截图后，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程与解决办法，员工确认关闭；不认可时可退回，工程师再次处理后关闭。工单状态历史完整可追溯。

        三类角色权限隔离

        员工列表只显示自己的工单，工程师只显示分派给自己的工单，服务台可见全部；员工/工程师直接访问他人或未分派工单均返回 Ticket not found。

        通知与跳转

        分派通知工程师、解决结果通知员工、退回通知工程师，点击通知可进入对应工单；首页展示待分派、超时工单与各工程师工作量。

        截图上传

        合法的 32x32 PNG 截图可上传、回显并保存为工单附件；不支持格式被明确拒绝。损坏 PNG 会被静默接受并显示为破损图片，作为观察项记录。

        01管理员登录并创建员工/工程师/服务台账号
        →
        02员工登录提交带截图的加急工单
        →
        03服务台查看全部工单并分派给工程师
        →
        04工程师收到通知并记录处理过程与解决办法
        →
        05员工收到待确认通知后先退回继续处理
        →
        06工程师再次解决，员工确认关闭
        →
        07服务台复核首页计数与超时提醒

        业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

        Acceptance record

        01

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        观察项（非验收要求）：损坏 PNG corrupt.png 被静默接受并在工单 #6 中渲染为 0x0 破损图片；不支持格式 unsupported.exe 被拒绝并提示 File type is not allowed

        A1 · 9 条操作 · 9 条观察 · 13 张证据
        Agent 报告通过
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。

        实际操作

        1. 用 admin/nocobase 登录，确认进入 IT service desk 业务界面
        2. 在 Team 页创建 qaemployee(无角色)、qaengineer(IT engineer)、qadesk(IT service desk) 三个账号
        3. 以 qaemployee 登录：工单列表为空，看不到 Employee One 的示例工单；直接打开 /main/tickets/1 返回 Ticket not found
        4. qaemployee 提交工单 #5：标题、描述、紧急程度 Urgent、上传 sample.png 截图后提交成功
        5. 以 qadesk 登录：首页显示待分派/进行中/待确认/已关闭计数、超时工单与各工程师工作量；工单列表可见全部 5 条；将 #5 分派给 QA Engineer（pending→processing）
        6. 以 qaengineer 登录：Inbox 收到分派通知，点击进入工单 #5；工单列表只显示分派给自己的 #5；直接打开 /main/tickets/1、/main/tickets/2 均返回 Ticket not found
        7. qaengineer 记录处理过程、记录解决办法并解决（processing→resolved）
        8. 以 qaemployee 登录：Inbox 收到待确认通知并点击进入 #5；先退回继续处理（resolved→processing，填写退回原因），工程师再次解决后确认关闭（resolved→closed）
        9. 以 qadesk 登录复核首页计数与超时提醒，检查浏览器控制台无报错

        观察结果

        1. admin 首页显示待分派 1、进行中 2、待确认 1、已关闭 0，超时区块显示 #3，工程师工作量 Engineer One 1 / Engineer Two 1
        2. qaemployee 首页计数全为 0，工单列表显示 No tickets；直接访问 /main/tickets/1 显示 Ticket not found
        3. 工单 #5 附件 sample.png 正常显示（img naturalWidth/Height=32），服务端文件与清单 sha256 eafcdecc... 一致
        4. 分派后 #5 Engineer=QA Engineer，历史 Assign pending→processing；工程师 Inbox 出现 A ticket was assigned to you 通知
        5. 点击通知进入 /main/tickets/5；工程师工单列表仅显示 #5，其他工程师/未分派工单不可见
        6. 工程师记录处理过程与解决办法，历史 Resolve processing→resolved
        7. 员工 Inbox 出现 Your ticket awaits confirmation；退回历史 Return for further handling resolved→processing，再次解决后 Confirm and close resolved→closed
        8. 服务台首页 Closed 计数变为 1，超时区块持续显示 #3；控制台无报错
        9. 观察项（非验收要求）：损坏 PNG corrupt.png 被静默接受并在工单 #6 中渲染为 0x0 破损图片；不支持格式 unsupported.exe 被拒绝并提示 File type is not allowed

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

        完整原始验收要求
